      "result": "{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"experiments\": {\"plan017-additive-random-s42\": {\"status\": \"ANALYZING\", \"created_at\": \"2026-05-14T03:11:14.694Z\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-14T05:17:14.092Z\", \"hypothesis\": \"Additive random control: r = r_exec·(1+0.3·random). 隔离 ISR structural signal vs variance regularization. 若 ISR > random > exec-only → signal+regularization 均贡献; 若 ISR > random ≈ exec-only → ISR signal 是关键; 若 ISR ≈ random → ISR 无结构价值。\", \"parent\": \"plan013-random-reward-s42-r4\"}, \"plan001-exec-only-s44\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"created_at\": \"2026-05-14T03:57:09.690Z\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-14T05:14:31.732Z\", \"hypothesis\": \"Exec-only seed=44 第3 seed: 解决 s42(93% eval PR) vs s43(13% eval PR) 的跨 seed 不稳定问题，3-seed mean±std 是论文必需。\", \"metrics\": {\"eval_prove_rate\": 0.98, \"eval_mean_isr\": 0.57, \"eval_isr_std\": 0.2013, \"eval_median_isr\": 0.5, \"eval_proved_only_isr\": 0.5816, \"eval_n_proved\": 98, \"eval_component_count_3\": 4, \"eval_by_type_false_pr\": 1, \"eval_by_type_false_isr\": 0.62, \"eval_by_type_unknown_pr\": 0.976, \"eval_by_type_unknown_isr\": 0.524, \"eval_by_type_true_pr\": 0.971, \"eval_by_type_true_isr\": 0.588, \"train_last100_pr\": 0.9988, \"train_last100_isr\": 0.5611, \"train_last100_loss\": 0.000419, \"train_duration_min\": 36.8, \"tautological_collapse\": true}, \"conclusion\": \"negative\", \"notes\": \"TAUTOLOGICAL COLLAPSE 确认。eval PR=98% 但 94% 样本仅 2 components (premise=conclusion tautology)，mean components=2.04，与 plan013 random reward 完全一致。ISR≈0.5 是 2-component artifact。s42 是唯一产生 genuine proofs (mean components=6.58) 的 seed。3-seed 结果：s42=genuine, s43=degraded(PR=13%), s44=tautological(PR=98% fake)。Exec-only reward 2/3 seeds 失败。\"}, \"plan014-downstream-nli-s42\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"created_at\": \"2026-05-14T03:43:37.925Z\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-14T04:29:48.645Z\", \"hypothesis\": \"Downstream NLI validation: 高ISR formalization 应在独立推理任务上表现更好(entailment accuracy)，Spearman ρ(ISR, accuracy) > 0.1 验证 ISR 作为 quality proxy 的实用价值。同时用 Z3 cross-solver agreement 验证 formalization 鲁棒性。\", \"notes\": \"Negative: ISR 作为 downstream NLI quality proxy 无效。Spearman ρ(ISR, correct)=-0.049 (p=0.187)，Best-of-N ISR 仅 +0.9pp over random (不显著)。主因：模型严重 true-bias (false accuracy=0%)，NLI accuracy 天花板仅 34.1%，ISR 无区分空间。ISR bucket 层面有弱单调趋势 (high=75% > mid=37.7% > low=32.7%) 但 high bucket 仅 12 样本。quartile 分析非单调。\", \"conclusion\": \"negative\", \"metrics\": {\"overall_accuracy\": 0.341, \"proved_rate\": 0.915, \"proved_count\": \\\"results\\\": results}\\n\\n    Path(args.output_path).parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\\n    with open(args.output_path, \\\"w\\\") as f:\\n        json.dump(output, f, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)\\n\\n    print(f\\\"\\\\n{'='*60}\\\")\\n    print(f\\\"Results saved to {args.output_path}\\\")\\n    print(f\\\"  Problems:     {n}\\\")\\n    print(f\\\"  Proved:       {proved_count}/{n} ({prove_rate:.1%})\\\")\\n    print(f\\\"  Mean ISR:     {mean_isr:.4f} +/- {std_isr:.4f}\\\")\\n    print(f\\\"  Median ISR:   {median_isr:.4f}\\\")\\n    print(f\\\"  ISR/PR ratio: {isr_pr_ratio:.4f}\\\")\\n    print(f\\\"  Proved-only ISR: {proved_mean_isr:.4f}\\\")\\n    print(f\\\"  By answer type:\\\")\\n    for a, s in answer_stats.items():\\n        print(f\\\"    {a}: n={s['count']}, proved={s['proved']}, ISR={s['mean_isr']:.4f}\\\")\\n    print(f\\\"  Time: {elapsed:.1f}s ({summary['avg_time_per_sample']:.1f}s/sample)\\\")\\n\\n\\nif __name__ ==   "account": "root"
}

The additive random noise appears to severely degrade both proof rate and structural efficiency, leaving the model generating unnecessarily complex proofs that it can't effectively learn from. Looking at the hypothesis framework, the results show random performing far worse than exec-only on proof rate, which suggests the ISR signal itself carries structural value that random noise can't replicate. The random variance control actually degrades performance compared to exec-only, which tells me that noise alone isn't a useful regularizer—ISR's benefit, if it exists, must come from its structural properties rather than just adding variance to the signal. ## plan017-additive-random-s42 评估结果

| 指标 | plan017 (additive random) | plan001-s42-r2 (exec-only) | plan013-r4 (pure random) | plan001-s44 (exec-only) |
|------|:---:|:---:|:---:|:---:|
| **Prove Rate** | 0.48 | 0.93 | 1.00 | 0.98 |
| **Mean ISR** | 0.0453 | 0.1232 | 0.535 | 0.57 |
| **Proved-only ISR** | 0.0943 | 0.1324 | 0.535 | 0.5816 |
| **Mean Components** | 9.56 | 6.58 | ≈2 | 2.04 |
| **2-comp %** | 0% | — | ~100% | 94% |
| **Collapse?** | **No** | **No** | **Yes** | **Yes** |

**核心发现：**

1. **No tautological collapse** — 0% 2-component, mean 9.56 components. Additive random noise prevents the collapse seen in plan013/plan001-s44.

2. **但 PR 和 ISR 都严重下降** — PR=0.48（远低于 exec-only 的 0.93），ISR=0.045（低于 exec-only 的 0.123）。Random noise 破坏了 exec reward signal。

3. **不同的 failure mode** — 模型生成复杂 formalization（9-18 components），但 33% proved 样本 n_necessary=0（conclusion 不依赖任何 premise），说明生成了大量无用 premises。

4. **对假设的回答** — random << exec-only，排除了"variance regularization alone is beneficial"假设。ISR（如果有效）的价值来自结构信号，不是 noise/variance。

Registry 已更新为 DONE/negative。
